const { PDFDocument } = require('pdf-lib');
const { isJSON } = require('../utils/constants');
 
// Generate parts consumed and returned table
function generatePartReturnedAndConsumedTable( param, defaultValue, isOnepmFSR=false) {
	let returnedEls = '';
	let issuedEls = '';
	let issuedCount = 0;
	let returnedCount = 0;
	let issueCount = 0;
	let returnCount = 0;
	if (param.material && param.material.length > 0) {
		//To restrict the number of parts to be displayed in the table for onepmFSR childs
		const materialArr = isOnepmFSR ? param.material.filter((element) => {
			if (element.part_activity.toLowerCase().includes("issued") && issueCount < 3) {
				issueCount++;
				return true;
			}
			if (element.part_activity.toLowerCase().includes("return") && returnCount < 3) {
				returnCount++;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}) : param.material;
 
		materialArr.forEach((element) => {
			if (element.part_activity.toLowerCase().includes('issued')) {
				issuedEls += `
				<div
							style="
								display: flex;
								flex-direction: row;
								border-bottom: 1px solid black;
								padding-left: 0.4rem;
								padding-right: 0.4rem;
								text-align: center;
								font-weight: 200;
							"
						>
							<div style="width: 5.33%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center;">${++issuedCount}</div>
							<div style="width: 13.6%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;">${element.part_code ? element.part_code : ""}</div>
							<div style="width: 49%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;">${element.part_description ? element.part_description : ""}</div>
							<div style="width: 23.6%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;">${element.part_serialno ? element.part_serialno : ""}</div>
							<div style="width: 8.33%; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem;">${element.part_qty ? element.part_qty : ""}</div>
						</div>
				`;
			}
 
			if (element.part_activity.toLowerCase().includes('return')) {
				returnedEls += `
				<div
							style="
								display: flex;
								flex-direction: row;
								border-bottom: 1px solid black;
								padding-left: 0.4rem;
								padding-right: 0.4rem;
								text-align: center;
								font-weight: 200;
							"
						>
							<div style="width: 5.33%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center;">${++returnedCount}</div>
							<div style="width: 13.6%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;">${element.part_code ? element.part_code : ""}</div>
							<div style="width: 49%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;">${element.part_description ? element.part_description : ""}</div>
							<div style="width: 23.6%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;">${element.part_serialno ? element.part_serialno : ""}</div>
							<div style="width: 8.33%; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem;">${element.part_qty ? element.part_qty : ""}</div>
						</div>
				`;
			}
		});
	}
 
	if (returnedCount < defaultValue || issuedCount < defaultValue) {
		for (let i = issuedCount; i < defaultValue; i++) {
			issuedEls += `
				<div
					style="
						display: flex;
						flex-direction: row;
						border-bottom: 1px solid black;
						padding-left: 0.4rem;
						padding-right: 0.4rem;
						text-align: center;
						font-weight: 200;
					"
				>
					<div style="width: 5.33%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center;">${i+1}</div>
					<div style="width: 13.6%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem;"></div>
					<div style="width: 49%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem;"></div>
					<div style="width: 23.6%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem;"></div>
					<div style="width: 8.33%; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem;"></div>
				</div>
			`;
		}
	
		for (let i = returnedCount; i < defaultValue; i++) {
			returnedEls += `
				<div
					style="
						display: flex;
						flex-direction: row;
						border-bottom: 1px solid black;
						padding-left: 0.4rem;
						padding-right: 0.4rem;
						text-align: center;
						font-weight: 200;
					"
				>
					<div style="width: 5.33%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center;">${i+1}</div>
					<div style="width: 13.6%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem;"></div>
					<div style="width: 49%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem;"></div>
					<div style="width: 23.6%; border-right: 1px solid black; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem;"></div>
					<div style="width: 8.33%; text-align: center; padding-left: 0.4rem;"></div>
				</div>
			`;
		}
	}
 
	return { issuedEls, returnedEls }
}
 
 
// Generate safety assessment table
function generateSafetyTable(formdata) {
	const organizedData = {};
 
	let hazardsHTML = '<div style="text-align: left; width: 32%;" class="assessmentItem">';
	let risksHTML = '<div  style="text-align: center;" class="assessmentItem">';
	let canProceedHTML = '<div  style="text-align: center;" class="assessmentItem">';
	let measuresHTML = '<div  style="text-align: center;" class="assessmentItem">';
 
	// Organize the data by hazard and attribute
	if (formdata && formdata.length > 0) {
		formdata.forEach((item) => {
			for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(item)) {
				const keyParts = key.split(' - ');
				const category = keyParts[0];
				const attribute = keyParts[1];
 
				if (!organizedData[category]) {
					organizedData[category] = {};
				}
 
				organizedData[category][attribute] = value;
			}
		});
 
		for (const hazard in organizedData) {
			hazardsHTML += `${hazard} <br />`;
			risksHTML += `${organizedData[hazard]['Level of Risk'] || ''} <br />`;
			canProceedHTML += `${organizedData[hazard]['Can work proceed safely?'] || ''} <br />`;
			measuresHTML += `${organizedData[hazard]['Detail safety measures put in place?'] || ''} <br />`;
		}
	}
 
	hazardsHTML += '</div>';
	risksHTML += '</div>';
	canProceedHTML += '</div>';
	measuresHTML += '</div>';
 
	return `
		<div class="assessmentHeader">
			<div class="assessmentItem" style="text-align: center; font-weight: bold;width: 32%; font-size: 11.5px; font-family: TimesBold;">Hazard <br /></div>
			<div  class="assessmentItem" style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px; font-family: TimesBold;">Level of Risk <br /></div>
			<div class="assessmentItem" style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px; font-family: TimesBold;">Can work proceed safely? <br /></div>
			<div  class="assessmentItem" style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px; font-family: TimesBold;">Safety measures put in place? <br /></div>
		</div>
		<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: row; font-size: 10; font-family: Times;">
			${hazardsHTML}
			${risksHTML}
			${canProceedHTML}
			${measuresHTML}
		</div>
	`;
}

  await page.evaluate((finalObject) => {
    function setData(el, data) {
      const element = document.getElementById(el);
      if (element) element.textContent = data || '';
    }
    function setImage(el, data) {
			document.getElementById(el).src = data;
		}
    function setHTML(el, data) {
      const element = document.getElementById(el);
      if (element) element.innerHTML = data || '';
    }
    const engineerSignature =
			finalObject.param?.engineerSignature && finalObject.param.engineerSignature.length > 1
				? finalObject.param.engineerSignature
				: finalObject.room &&
					finalObject.room?.customFields &&
					finalObject.room?.customFields?.engineerSignature &&
					finalObject.room.customFields.engineerSignature.length > 1
					? finalObject.room.customFields.engineerSignature
					: '';
 
		const managerSignature =
			finalObject.param?.managerSignature && finalObject.param.managerSignature.length > 1
				? finalObject.param.managerSignature
				: finalObject.room &&
					finalObject.room?.customFields &&
					finalObject.room?.customFields?.managerSignature &&
					finalObject.room.customFields.managerSignature.length > 1
					? finalObject.room.customFields.managerSignature
					: '';
 
    // Basic customer and call information
    setData('customerName', finalObject.param.customer_name);
    setData('fsrNumber', finalObject.param.call_no);
    setData('fsrDateAndTime', finalObject.param.completion_date);
    setData('serviceType', finalObject.param.servicetype);
    
    // Customer address and contact information
    const customerAddress = [finalObject.param.customer_address1, finalObject.param.customer_address2, finalObject.param.customer_address3]
      .filter(addr => addr && addr.trim())
      .join(', ');
    setData('customerAddress', customerAddress);
    setData('contactPerson', finalObject.param.contact);
    setData('contactNumber', finalObject.param.contact_no);
    
    // Product information
    setData('productModel', finalObject.param.product_model);
    setData('productRating', finalObject.param.product_rating);
    setData('serialNumber', finalObject.param.product_serialno);
    setData('productCoverage', finalObject.param.product_coverage);
    
    // Engineer information
    setData('engineerName', finalObject.param.engineername);
    setData('engineerContactNumber', finalObject.param.call_engineer_mobilenumber);
    
    // Request and timing information
    setData('requestNumber', finalObject.param.id);
    setData('requestDateAndTime', finalObject.param.call_log_date);
    setData('assignedDateTime', finalObject.param.call_log_date);
    setData('scheduledDateTime', finalObject.param.call_log_date);
    setData('closedDateTime', finalObject.param.call_actual_end_date);
    
    // Problem statement and call details
    setData('problemStatement', finalObject.param.problemstatement);
    setData('callType', finalObject.param.call_type);
    setData('problemCode', finalObject.param.problem_code_description);
    setData('resolutionCode', finalObject.param.resolution_code_description);
    
    // Timing information
    setData('travelStartDateAndTime', finalObject.param.travel_start_time);
    setData('reportingDateAndTime', finalObject.param.reporting_date);
    setData('completionDateAndTime', finalObject.param.completion_date);
    setData('onSiteTime', finalObject.param.on_site_time);
    setData('travelTime', finalObject.param.travel_time);
    setData('numberOfVisits', finalObject.param.visits);
    setData('equipmentFace', finalObject.param.equipment_facetime_info);
    setData('breakTime', finalObject.param.break_time);
    setData('totalTimeSpent', finalObject.param.total_time);
    
    // Service billing
    setData('serviceBillable', finalObject.param.serviceBillable || 'Yes');
 
    let signatureHeader = `
					<div
						style="
							display: flex;
							flex-direction: column;
						"
					>
						<div style="width: 99%; text-align: justify; padding-left: 0.2rem; padding-right: 0.2rem; border-bottom: 1px solid black" class="times-bold">Customer's Comment</div>
						<div style="width: 99%; text-align: justify; padding-left: 0.2rem; padding-right: 0.2rem;  height: 4.5rem; font-weight: normal;">${finalObject?.paramObj?.ratings?.comment ? finalObject?.paramObj?.ratings?.comment : ''}</div>
					</div>
		`
 
		let signatureContent = `
			<div
				style="
					display: flex;
					flex-direction: row;
					text-align: center;
				"
			>
				<div style="width: ${managerSignature ? '33.33%' : '50%'}; flex-direction: column; height: inherit; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; text-align: center;">
					${finalObject.paramObj?.signature ? `<img src="${finalObject.paramObj?.signature}" id="customerSignatureAndSeal" class="full" />` : `<div class='full'></div>`}
					<div class="times-bold">Customer Signature</div>
				</div>
				<div style="width: ${managerSignature ? '33.33%' : '50%'}; flex-direction: column; height: inherit; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; text-align: center;  border-right: ${managerSignature ? '1px' : '0px'} solid black; border-left: 1px solid black;">
					${engineerSignature ? `<img src="${engineerSignature}" id="engineerSignatureAndSeal" class="full" />` : `<div class='full'></div>`}
					<div class="times-bold">Engineer Signature</div>
				</div>
				${managerSignature ?
				`<div style="width: 33.33%; flex-direction: column; height: inherit; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; text-align: center;">
						<img src="${managerSignature}" class="full" />
						<div class="times-bold">Signature of Manager</div>
					</div>`
				: ''
			}
			</div>
		`
 
		setHTML('signatureHeader', signatureHeader);
		setHTML('signatureContent', signatureContent);
 
    // Process workbench activities
    let obContent = '';
    let wdContent = '';
    let reContent = '';
 
    if (finalObject.param.workbench && Array.isArray(finalObject.param.workbench)) {
      finalObject.param.workbench.forEach(workbenchObj => {
        if (workbenchObj.activity_type_value?.toLowerCase().indexOf('observation') !== -1) {
          if (workbenchObj.activity_notes?.length > 0) {
            obContent = obContent.length > 0 ? `${obContent}\n` : obContent;
            obContent = `${obContent}[${workbenchObj.activity_date}] ${workbenchObj.activity_notes}`;
          }
        } else if (workbenchObj.activity_type_value?.toLowerCase().indexOf('recommendation') !== -1) {
          Eif (workbenchObj.activity_notes?.length > 0) {
            reContent = reContent.length > 0 ? `${reContent}\n` : reContent;
            reContent = `${reContent}[${workbenchObj.activity_date}] ${workbenchObj.activity_notes}`;
          }
        } else Eif (workbenchObj.activity_type_value?.toLowerCase().indexOf('work done') !== -1) {
          if (workbenchObj.activity_notes?.length > 0) {
            wdContent = wdContent.length > 0 ? `${wdContent}\n` : wdContent;
            wdContent = `${wdContent}[${workbenchObj.activity_date}] ${workbenchObj.activity_notes}`;
          }
        }
      });
    }
 
    // Set workbench content (these elements may not exist in template, but keeping for compatibility)
    setHTML('observation', obContent.replace(/\n/g, "<br><div style='height: 5px;'></div>"));
    setHTML('workDone', wdContent.replace(/\n/g, "<br><div style='height: 5px;'></div>"));
    setHTML('recommendation', reContent.replace(/\n/g, "<br><div style='height: 5px;'></div>"));
    
    // Set parts and assessment tables
    setHTML('partConsumed', finalObject.issuedEls);
    setHTML('partReturned', finalObject.returnedEls);
    setHTML('assessment', finalObject.tableHTML);
    
  }, finalObject);
 
  return await page.pdf({
    margin: { top: '5mm', right: '5mm', bottom: '5mm', left: '5mm' },
    format: 'A4',
    printBackground: true,
    displayHeaderFooter: true,
    headerTemplate: headerTemplate,
    footerTemplate: '<div style="font-size: 10px; font-family: TimesBold; text-align: right; margin-left: auto; padding-right: 30px; padding-bottom: 10px;">Page <span class="pageNumber"></span> of <span class="totalPages"></span></div>',
  });
}

	await page.evaluate((finalObject) => {
		function setData(el, data) {
			document.getElementById(el).textContent = data;
		}
 
		function setImage(el, data) {
			document.getElementById(el).src = data;
		}
 
		function setHTML(el, data) {
			document.getElementById(el).innerHTML = data;
		}
 
		const engineerSignature =
			finalObject.param?.engineerSignature && finalObject.param.engineerSignature.length > 1
				? finalObject.param.engineerSignature
				: finalObject.room &&
					finalObject.room?.customFields &&
					finalObject.room?.customFields?.engineerSignature &&
					finalObject.room.customFields.engineerSignature.length > 1
					? finalObject.room.customFields.engineerSignature
					: '';
 
		const managerSignature =
			finalObject.param?.managerSignature && finalObject.param.managerSignature.length > 1
				? finalObject.param.managerSignature
				: finalObject.room &&
					finalObject.room?.customFields &&
					finalObject.room?.customFields?.managerSignature &&
					finalObject.room.customFields.managerSignature.length > 1
					? finalObject.room.customFields.managerSignature
					: '';
 
		const partner_logo =
			finalObject.param?.partner_logo && finalObject.param.partner_logo.length > 1
				? finalObject.param.partner_logo
				: finalObject.room &&
					finalObject.room?.customFields &&
					finalObject.room?.customFields?.partner_logo &&
					finalObject.room.customFields.partner_logo.length > 1
					? finalObject.room.customFields.partner_logo
					: '';
		const partner_organization = finalObject.param?.partner_organization
			? finalObject.param.partner_organization
			: finalObject.room?.customFields && finalObject.room?.customFields?.partner_organization
				? finalObject.room.customFields.partner_organization
				: '';
		const partner_branchaddress = finalObject.param?.partner_branchaddress
			? finalObject.param.partner_branchaddress
			: finalObject.room?.customFields && finalObject.room?.customFields?.partner_branchaddress
				? finalObject.room.customFields.partner_branchaddress
				: '';
		const partner_city = finalObject.param?.partner_city
			? finalObject.param.partner_city
			: finalObject.room?.customFields && finalObject.room?.customFields?.partner_city
				? finalObject.room.customFields.partner_city
				: '';
		const partner_contact_number = finalObject.param?.partner_contact_number
			? finalObject.param.partner_contact_number
			: finalObject.room?.customFields && finalObject.room?.customFields?.partner_contact_number
				? finalObject.room.customFields.partner_contact_number
				: '';
 
		setData('customerName', finalObject.param.customer_name || '');
		setData(
			'customerAddress',
			`${finalObject.param.customer_address1 ? finalObject.param.customer_address1 : ""}${finalObject.param.customer_address2 ? ', ' + finalObject.param.customer_address2 : ""}${finalObject.param.customer_address3 ? ', ' +  finalObject.param.customer_address3 : ""}`,
		);

		setData('engineerContactNumber', finalObject.param.call_engineer_mobilenumber || '');

		setData('productRating', finalObject.param.product_rating || '');
		setData('serialNumber', finalObject.param.product_serialno || '');
		setData('serviceType', finalObject.param.servicetype || '');
		setData('fsrNumber', finalObject.paramObj.fsr_number || '');
		setData('problemCode', finalObject.param.problem_code_description || '');
		setData('resolutionCode', finalObject.param.resolution_code_description || '');
		setData('callType', finalObject.param.call_type || '');
		setData('problemStatement', finalObject.param.problemstatement || '');
		setData('travelStartDateAndTime', finalObject.param.travel_start_time || '');
		setData('reportingDateAndTime', finalObject.param.reporting_date || '');
		setData('completionDateAndTime', finalObject.param.completion_date || '');
		setData('onSiteTime', finalObject.param.on_site_time || '');
		setData('travelTime', finalObject.param.travel_time || '');
		setData('numberOfVisits', finalObject.param.visits || '');
		setData('equipmentFace', finalObject.param.equipment_facetime_info || '');
		setData('breakTime', finalObject.param.break_time || '');
		setData('totalTimeSpent', finalObject.param.total_time || '');
		setHTML('assessment', finalObject.tableHTML || '');
 
		setData('partnerOrganizationName', partner_organization);
		setData('partnerAddress', partner_branchaddress);
		setData('partnerCity', partner_city);
		setData('partnerContactNo', partner_contact_number);
		setData('requestNumber', finalObject.param.call_no || '');
		setData('requestDateAndTime', finalObject.param.call_log_date || '');
		setData('closedDateAndTime', finalObject.param.call_actual_end_date || '');
		setData('dgBackup', finalObject?.paramObj?.dpg_data?.dg_backup ? finalObject?.paramObj?.dpg_data?.dg_backup : '');
		setData('configuration', finalObject.paramObj?.dpg_data?.configuration_type ? finalObject?.paramObj?.dpg_data?.configuration_type : '');
		setData('loadType', finalObject.paramObj?.dpg_data?.load_type ? finalObject?.paramObj?.dpg_data?.load_type : '');
		setData('productCoverage', finalObject.param.product_coverage ? finalObject.param.product_coverage : '');
 
		setData('serviceBillable', finalObject.param.serviceBillable || '');
 
		let partnerLogo = `
		<img src="${partner_logo}" id="logo" style="min-width:${!partner_logo ? '0' : '50%'}; min-height: ${!partner_logo ? '0' : '50%'}; max-width:${!partner_logo ? '0' : '50%'}; max-height: ${!partner_logo ? '0' : '50%'};" /> <br />
		`
		setHTML('partnerLogo',partnerLogo);
 
		let signatureHeader = `
					<div
						style="
							display: flex;
							flex-direction: column;
						"
					>
						<div style="width: 99%; text-align: justify; padding-left: 0.2rem; padding-right: 0.2rem; border-bottom: 1px solid black" class="times-bold">Customer Comment</div>
						<div style="width: 99%; text-align: justify; padding-left: 0.2rem; padding-right: 0.2rem;  height: 4.5rem; font-weight: normal;">${finalObject?.paramObj?.ratings?.comment ? finalObject?.paramObj?.ratings?.comment : ''}</div>
					</div>
		`
 
		let signatureContent = `
			<div
				style="
					display: flex;
					flex-direction: row;
					text-align: center;
				"
			>
				<div style="width: ${managerSignature ? '33.33%' : '50%'}; flex-direction: column; height: inherit; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; text-align: center;">

					<div class="times-bold">Customer Signature</div>
				</div>
				<div style="width: ${managerSignature ? '33.33%' : '50%'}; flex-direction: column; height: inherit; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; text-align: center;  border-right: ${managerSignature ? '1px' : '0px'} solid black; border-left: 1px solid black;">
					${engineerSignature ? `<img src="${engineerSignature}" id="engineerSignatureAndSeal" class="full" />` : `<div class='full'></div>`}
					<div class="times-bold">Engineer Signature</div>
				</div>

				`<div style="width: 33.33%; flex-direction: column; height: inherit; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; text-align: center;">
						<img src="${managerSignature}" class="full" />

				: ''

			</div>

		setHTML('signatureContent', signatureContent);
 
		let obContent = '';
		let wdContent = '';
		let reContent = '';
 
		for (let i = 0; i < finalObject.param.workbench.length; i++) {
			const workbenchObj = finalObject.param.workbench[i];
			if (workbenchObj.activity_type_value?.toLowerCase().indexOf('observation') !== -1) {
				if (workbenchObj.activity_notes?.length > 0) {
					obContent = obContent.length > 0 ? `${obContent}\n` : obContent;
					obContent = `${obContent}[${workbenchObj.activity_date}] &nbsp;${workbenchObj.activity_notes}`;
				}
			} else if (workbenchObj.activity_type_value?.toLowerCase().indexOf('recommendation') !== -1) {
				if (workbenchObj.activity_notes?.length > 0) {
					reContent = reContent.length > 0 ? `${reContent}\n` : reContent;
					reContent = `${reContent}[${workbenchObj.activity_date}] &nbsp;${workbenchObj.activity_notes}`;
				}
			} else if (workbenchObj.activity_type_value?.toLowerCase().indexOf('work done') !== -1) {
				if (workbenchObj.activity_notes?.length > 0) {
					wdContent = wdContent.length > 0 ? `${wdContent}\n` : wdContent;
					wdContent = `${wdContent}[${workbenchObj.activity_date}] &nbsp;${workbenchObj.activity_notes}`;
				}
			}
 
			setHTML('observation', obContent.replace(/\n/g, "<br><div style='height: 5px;'></div>"));
			setHTML('workDone', wdContent.replace(/\n/g, "<br><div style='height: 5px;'></div>"));
			setHTML('recommendation', reContent.replace(/\n/g, "<br><div style='height: 5px;'></div>"));
		}
 
		setData('logo', finalObject.logo || '');
 
		setHTML('partConsumed', finalObject.issuedEls || '');
		setHTML('partReturned', finalObject.returnedEls || '');
 
		const { dpg_data } = finalObject.paramObj;
 
		dpg_data?.power_parameters.forEach((param) => {
			for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(param)) {
				const unit = key.includes('voltage') ? 'V' : 'A';
				for (const [subKey, subValue] of Object.entries(value)) {
					const elementId = subKey;
					const element = document.getElementById(elementId);
					if (element) {
						element.textContent = `${elementId.split('_').pop().toUpperCase()}: ${subValue}${unit}`;
					}
				}
			}
		});
 
		setData('n_e_voltage', dpg_data?.n_e_voltage ?  dpg_data?.n_e_voltage : '');
		setData('i_p_frequency', dpg_data?.i_p_frequency ? `${dpg_data?.i_p_frequency}Hz` : '');
		setData('o_p_frequency', dpg_data?.o_p_frequency ? `${dpg_data?.o_p_frequency}Hz` : '');
 
		setData('floatVoltage', dpg_data?.float_voltage ? dpg_data?.float_voltage : '');
		setData('batteryMake', dpg_data?.battery_make ? dpg_data?.battery_make : '');
		setData('batteryType', dpg_data?.battery_type ? dpg_data?.battery_type : '');
		setData('batteryCapacity', dpg_data?.battery_capacity ? dpg_data?.battery_capacity : '');
		setData('batteryQty', dpg_data?.battery_quantity ? dpg_data?.battery_quantity : '');
		setData('chargingCurrent', dpg_data?.charging_current ? dpg_data?.charging_current : '');
		setData('batteryRoomTemperature', dpg_data?.battery_room_temperature ? `${dpg_data?.battery_room_temperature}°C`: '');
		setData('upsRoomTemperature', dpg_data?.ups_room_temperature ? `${dpg_data?.ups_room_temperature}°C`: '');
	}, finalObject);
	return await page.pdf({
		margin: {
			top: '5mm',
			right: '5mm',
			bottom: '5mm',
			left: '5mm',
		},
		format: 'A4',
		printBackground: true,
		displayHeaderFooter: true,
		headerTemplate: css + headerTemplate,
		footerTemplate: css + '<div style="font-size: 10px; font-family: TimesBold; text-align: right; margin-left: auto; padding-right: 30px; padding-bottom: 10px;">Page <span class="pageNumber"></span> of 2</div>',
	});
};
 
// Generate DCPS Report
const generateDCPSReport = async (page, finalObject) => {
	const headerTemplate = 
		`<div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; width: 100%">
			<div style="display: flex; padding: 0.5rem; margin-left: 30px; margin-top: 10px;">
				<img
					height="30px"
					src="PLACEHOLDER_LOGO_IMAGE"
				/>
			</div>
			<div style="font-size: 21px; font-family: TimesBold; margin-right: 30px; margin-top: 7px;">Field Service Report</div>
		</div>`
	const css = 
		`<style>
			@font-face {
				font-family: TimesBold;
				src: url(data:font/truetype;charset=utf-8;base64,PLACEHOLDER_FONT_DATA);
			}
		</style>`
 
	finalObject.batteryDetails = {};
	finalObject.riskAssesment = {};
	finalObject.param.cms_dcps_value = isJSON(finalObject.param.cms_dcps_value) ? JSON.parse(finalObject.param.cms_dcps_value) : {};

	if (finalObject.paramObj.formdata && Array.isArray(finalObject.paramObj.formdata)) {
		finalObject.riskAssesment = generateDCPSRiskAssesment(finalObject.paramObj.formdata);
	}
 
	await page.evaluate((finalObject) => {
		function setData(el, data) {
			document.getElementById(el).textContent = data;

			document.getElementById(el).src = data;
		}

		}
 
		const engineerSignature =
			finalObject.param?.engineerSignature && finalObject.param.engineerSignature.length > 1

					? finalObject.paramObj.engineerSignature

						finalObject.room?.customFields &&

					finalObject.room?.customFields &&

				? finalObject.room.customFields.partner_branchaddress
				: '';

		setData('controllerModelName', finalObject.param.cms_dcps_value._controller_model || '-');

		setData('reportingDateAndTime', finalObject.param.reporting_date || '');
		setData('completionDateAndTime', finalObject.param.completion_date || '');
		setData('onSiteTime', finalObject.param.on_site_time || '');
		setData('travelTime', finalObject.param.travel_time || '');
		setData('numberOfVisits', finalObject.param.visits || '');
		setData('equipmentFace', finalObject.param.equipment_facetime_info || '');
		setData('breakTime', finalObject.param.break_time || '');
		setData('totalTimeSpent', finalObject.param.total_time || '');
 
		//Risk Assesment
		if (finalObject.riskAssesment && Object.values(finalObject.riskAssesment).length) {
			Object.entries(finalObject.riskAssesment).forEach(([key, value]) => {

		//Battery Details
		if (finalObject.batteryDetails && Object.values(finalObject.batteryDetails).length) {
			Object.entries(finalObject.batteryDetails).forEach(([key, value]) => {

			})
		}
 
		let partnerLogo = `<img src="${partner_logo}" id="logo" style="min-width:${!partner_logo ? '0' : '100%'}; min-height: ${!partner_logo ? '0' : '100%'}; max-width:${!partner_logo ? '0' : '100%'}; max-height: ${!partner_logo ? '0' : '100%'};" />`

		let signatureHeader = `
			<div
				style="
					display: flex;
					flex-direction: row;

			>

				</div>

				</div>

				style="

// Helper functions for DCPS
function generateBatteryDetails(batteryArray) {
	const batteryDetails = {};
	batteryArray.forEach((battery, index) => {
		batteryDetails[`battery${index + 1}Model`] = battery.model || '';
		batteryDetails[`battery${index + 1}Capacity`] = battery.capacity || '';
		batteryDetails[`battery${index + 1}Voltage`] = battery.voltage || '';
		batteryDetails[`battery${index + 1}Count`] = battery.count || '';
	});
	return batteryDetails;
}
 
function generateDCPSRiskAssesment(formdataArray) {
	const riskAssesment = {};
	formdataArray.forEach((form, index) => {
		if (form.risk_assessment) {
			Object.entries(form.risk_assessment).forEach(([key, value]) => {
				riskAssesment[`risk${index + 1}${key}`] = value || '';
			});
		}
	});
	return riskAssesment;
}
 
// Merge PDFs function
async function mergePDFs(bufferArray) {
    const mergedPdf = await PDFDocument.create();
    let currentPage = 1;
 
    // Add pages from each PDF
    for (let i = 0; i < bufferArray.length; i++) {
        try {
            const pdfDoc = await PDFDocument.load(bufferArray[i].buffer);
            const pages = await mergedPdf.copyPages(pdfDoc, pdfDoc.getPageIndices());
            for (let j = 0; j < pages.length; j++) {
                mergedPdf.addPage(pages[j]);
                currentPage++;
            }
        } catch (error) {
            console.error('Error during PDF merging:', error);
        }
    }
 
    // Serialize the merged PDF to a buffer (Uint8Array)
    const mergedPdfBuffer = await mergedPdf.save();
 
    // Convert Uint8Array to Node.js Buffer and return
    return Buffer.from(mergedPdfBuffer);
}
 
// Generate Safety Table for OnePM FSR
function generateSafetyTableForOnepmFSR(formdata) {
	const organizedData = {};
 
	let hazardsHTML = '<div style="text-align: left; width: 32%; padding: unset;" class="assessmentItem">';
	let risksHTML = '<div  style="text-align: center; padding: unset;" class="assessmentItem">';
	let canProceedHTML = '<div  style="text-align: center; padding: unset;" class="assessmentItem">';
	let measuresHTML = '<div  style="text-align: center; padding: unset;" class="assessmentItem">';
 
	// Organize the data by hazard and attribute
	if (formdata && formdata.length > 0) {
		formdata.forEach((item) => {
			for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(item)) {
				const keyParts = key.split(' - ');
				const category = keyParts[0];
				const attribute = keyParts[1];
 
				Eif (!organizedData[category]) {
					organizedData[category] = {};
				}
 
				organizedData[category][attribute] = value;
			}
		});
 
		const length = Object.keys(organizedData).length;
		let index = 0
		for (const hazard in organizedData) {
			hazardsHTML += `<div style="padding-left: 0.3rem; word-spacing: 1.5px; ${length - 1 !== index ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}">${hazard} </div>`;
			risksHTML += `<div style="padding-left: 0.3rem; word-spacing: 1.5px; ${length - 1 !== index ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}">${organizedData[hazard]['Level of Risk'] || ''} </div>`;
			canProceedHTML += `<div style="padding-left: 0.3rem; word-spacing: 1.5px; ${length - 1 !== index ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}">${organizedData[hazard]['Can work proceed safely?'] || ''} </div>`;
			measuresHTML += `<div style="padding-left: 0.3rem; word-spacing: 1.5px; ${length - 1 !== index ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}">${organizedData[hazard]['Detail safety measures put in place?'] || ''} </div>`;
			index++;
		}
	}
 
	hazardsHTML += '</div>';
	risksHTML += '</div>';
	canProceedHTML += '</div>';
	measuresHTML += '</div>';
 
	return `
		<div class="assessmentHeader">
			<div class="assessmentItem" style="text-align: center; font-weight: bold;width: 32%; font-size: 11.5px; font-family: TimesBold; padding: unset;">Hazard <br /></div>
			<div  class="assessmentItem" style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px; font-family: TimesBold; padding: unset;">Level of Risk <br /></div>
			<div class="assessmentItem" style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px; font-family: TimesBold; padding: unset;">Can work proceed safely? <br /></div>
			<div  class="assessmentItem" style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px; font-family: TimesBold; padding: unset;">Safety measures put in place? <br /></div>
		</div>
		<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: row; font-size: 10; font-family: Times;">
			${hazardsHTML}
			${risksHTML}
			${canProceedHTML}
			${measuresHTML}
		</div>
	`;
}
 
 
// Generate Products Covered Table
function generateProductsCoveredTable(summaryData, defaultLength=8) {
	const marginObj = {"12": "5.4rem", "13": "4rem", "14": "2.4rem", "15": "1.2rem", "17": "1.2rem","18": "1.2rem", "19": "1.2rem", "20": "1.2rem" }
	const organizedData = {};
	let pgNoHTML = '<div style="text-align: center; width: 4%; padding: unset; border-right: unset;" class="assessmentItem">';
	let requestNumberHTML = '<div  style="text-align: center; width: 15%; padding: unset; border-right: unset;" class="assessmentItem">';
	let productModelHTML = '<div  style="text-align: center; width: 23.5%; padding: unset; border-right: unset;" class="assessmentItem">';
	let productRatingHTML = '<div  style="text-align: center; width: 12%; padding: unset; border-right: unset;" class="assessmentItem">';
	let productSerialNoHTML = '<div  style="text-align: center; width: 22.5%; padding: unset; border-right: unset;" class="assessmentItem">';
	let productResolutionCodeHTML = '<div  style="text-align: center; width: 25%; padding: unset; border-right: unset;" class="assessmentItem">';
 
	if (summaryData && summaryData.length > 0) {
		let pageNoCount = 2;
		for (i=0; i < summaryData.length; i++) {
			pgNoHTML += `<div style="border-right: 1px solid black; width: 100%; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;min-height: 1.4rem; max-height: 1.4rem; ${defaultLength - 1 >  i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}">${pageNoCount}/${summaryData.length + 1} </div>`
			requestNumberHTML += `<div style="border-right: 1px solid black; width: 100%; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;min-height: 1.4rem; max-height: 1.4rem; ${defaultLength - 1 > i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}">${summaryData[i]?.call_no ? summaryData[i].call_no : ""} </div>`
			productModelHTML += `<div style="border-right: 1px solid black; width: 100%; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;min-height: 1.4rem; max-height: 1.4rem; ${defaultLength - 1 > i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}">${summaryData[i]?.product_model ? summaryData[i].product_model : ""} </div>`
			productRatingHTML += `<div style="border-right: 1px solid black; width: 100%; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;min-height: 1.4rem; max-height: 1.4rem; ${defaultLength - 1 > i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}">${summaryData[i]?.product_rating ? summaryData[i].product_rating : ""} </div>`
			productSerialNoHTML += `<div style="border-right: 1px solid black; width: 101%; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;min-height: 1.4rem; max-height: 1.4rem; ${defaultLength - 1 > i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}">${summaryData[i]?.product_serialno ? summaryData[i].product_serialno : ""} </div>`
			productResolutionCodeHTML += `<div style="width: 100%; text-overflow: ellipsis; display: -webkit-box; -webkit-line-clamp: 1; -webkit-box-orient: vertical; overflow: hidden;min-height: 1.4rem; max-height: 1.4rem; ${defaultLength - 1 > i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}">${summaryData[i]?.resolution_code_description ? summaryData[i].resolution_code_description : ""} </div>`
			pageNoCount+=1;
			if (i === 7) { // table lenght should not exceed 8
				break;
			}
		}
 
		if (summaryData.length < defaultLength) {
			for (i=summaryData.length; i < defaultLength; i++) {
				pgNoHTML += `<div style="border-right: 1px solid black; width: 100%;  ${defaultLength - 1 > i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}"> - </div>`
				requestNumberHTML += `<div style="border-right: 1px solid black; width: 100%;  ${defaultLength - 1 > i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}"> - </div>`
				productModelHTML += `<div style="border-right: 1px solid black; width:100%;  ${defaultLength - 1 > i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}"> - </div>`
				productRatingHTML += `<div style="border-right: 1px solid black; width: 100%;  ${defaultLength - 1 > i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}"> - </div>`
				productSerialNoHTML += `<div style="border-right: 1px solid black; width: 101%;  ${defaultLength - 1 > i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}"> - </div>`
				productResolutionCodeHTML += `<div style="width: 100%;  ${defaultLength - 1 > i ? "border-bottom: 1px solid black;": ""}"> - </div>`
			}
 
		}
	}
 
	pgNoHTML += '</div>';
	requestNumberHTML += '</div>';
	productModelHTML += '</div>';
	productRatingHTML += '</div>';
	productSerialNoHTML += '</div>';
	productResolutionCodeHTML += '</div>';
 
	return `
		<div class="assessmentHeader" style="width: 100%;">
			<div class="assessmentItem" style="text-align: center; font-weight: bold; width: 3.9%; font-size: 11.5px; font-family: TimesBold; padding: unset;">Pg# <br /></div>
			<div  class="assessmentItem" style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px; width: 15%; font-family: TimesBold; padding: unset;">Request Number<br /></div>
			<div class="assessmentItem" style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px; width: 23.5%; font-family: TimesBold; padding: unset;">Product Model <br /></div>
			<div  class="assessmentItem" style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px; font-family: TimesBold; width: 12%; padding: unset;">Product Rating <br /></div>
			<div  class="assessmentItem" style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px; width: 22.6%; font-family: TimesBold; padding: unset;">Product Serial No. <br /></div>
			<div  class="assessmentItem" style="font-weight: bold; font-size: 11.5px; font-family: TimesBold; width: 25%; padding: unset;">Resolution Code <br /></div>
		</div>
		<div style="display: flex; flex-direction: row; font-size: 9px; font-family: Times;" class="assessmentBody">
			${pgNoHTML}
			${requestNumberHTML}
			${productModelHTML}
			${productRatingHTML}
			${productSerialNoHTML}
			${productResolutionCodeHTML}
		</div>`;
}
 
// Generate OnePM FSR Master Report
const generateOnePMFSR = async (page, finalObject, totalPageNumber) => {
	await page.evaluate(({ finalObject, totalPageNumber }) => {
        function setData(el, data) {
            document.getElementById(el).textContent = data;
        }
 
        function setImage(el, data) {
            document.getElementById(el).src = data;
        }
 
        function setHTML(el, data) {
            document.getElementById(el).innerHTML = data;
        }
 
		setData('fsrNumber', finalObject.param?.fsr_number ? finalObject.param.fsr_number : finalObject?.paramObj && finalObject.paramObj?.fsr_number ?  finalObject?.paramObj?.fsr_number : '');
		setData('fsrDateAndTime', finalObject.param.completion_date || '');
		setData('serviceType', finalObject.param.servicetype || '');
		setData('serviceProvider', finalObject.param.service_provider || '');
		// setData('serviceBillable', finalObject.param.fsr_number || '');
		setData('problemCode', finalObject.param.problem_code_description || '');
		setData('customerName', finalObject.param.customer_name || '');
		setData(
			'customerAddress',
			`${finalObject.param.customer_address1 ? finalObject.param.customer_address1 : ""}${finalObject.param.customer_address2 ? ', ' + finalObject.param.customer_address2 : ""}${finalObject.param.customer_address3 ? ', ' +  finalObject.param.customer_address3 : ""}`,
		);
		setData('contactPerson', finalObject.param.contact || '');
		setData('contactNumber', finalObject.param.contact_no || '');
		setData('branch', finalObject.param.servicebranch || '');
		setData('zone', finalObject.param.zone || '');
		setData('bookedBy', finalObject?.booked_by ? finalObject?.booked_by : finalObject.param?.booked_by || '');
		setData('loggedThrough', finalObject?.logged_through ? finalObject?.logged_through : finalObject.param.logged_through || '');
		setData('requestOwner', finalObject?.request_owner ? finalObject?.request_owner : finalObject.param?.request_owner || '');
		setData('siteEngineer', finalObject?.engineername ? finalObject?.engineername : finalObject.param?.engineername || '');
		setData('engineerContact', finalObject.param.call_engineer_mobilenumber || '');
		setHTML('assessment', finalObject.tableHTML || '');
		setHTML('productsCovered', finalObject.productsCoveredHTML || '');
		setHTML('pageNo', `<div><b>Page 1 of</b></div><div><b>${totalPageNumber}</b></div>`);
		
		let signatureHeader = `
			<div
				style="
					display: flex;
					flex-direction: row;
				"
			>
				<div style="width: 50%; text-align: justify; padding-left: 0.2rem; padding-right: 0.2rem; border-right: 1px solid black;">
					<span class="times-bold">Customer Comment:</span>
					<div style="width: 99%; text-align: justify; padding-left: 0.2rem; padding-right: 0.2rem;  height: 1.5rem; font-weight: normal;">${finalObject?.paramObj?.ratings?.comment ? finalObject?.paramObj?.ratings?.comment : ''}</div>
				</div>
				<div style="width: 50%; text-align: justify; padding-left: 0.2rem; padding-right: 0.2rem;">
					<span class="times-bold">Site Engineer's Comment:</span>
					<div style="width: 99%; text-align: justify; padding-left: 0.2rem; padding-right: 0.2rem;  height: 1.5rem; font-weight: normal;">${finalObject?.engineerComment ? finalObject?.engineerComment : ''}</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		`
 
		let signatureContent = `
			<div
			style="
				display: flex;
				flex-direction: row;
				text-align: center;
			"
			>
				<div style="width: 50%; flex-direction: column; height: inherit; display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">
					${finalObject?.customerSignature ? ` <img src="${finalObject?.customerSignature}" id="customerSignatureAndSeal" class="full" /> ` : `<div class='full'></div>`}
					<div class="times-bold" style="border-top: 1px solid black;">Customer Signature</div>
				</div>
				<div style="width: 50%; flex-direction: column; height: inherit; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; border-right: '0px' solid black; border-left: 1px solid black;">
					${finalObject?.engineerSignature ? `<img src="${finalObject?.engineerSignature}" id="engineerSignatureAndSeal" class="full" />` : `<div class='full'></div>`}
					<div class="times-bold" style="border-top: 1px solid black;">Engineer Signature</div>
				</div>
			</div>
		`
 
		setHTML('signatureHeader', signatureHeader);
		setHTML('signatureContent', signatureContent);
	}, { finalObject, totalPageNumber });
 
	return await page.pdf({
        margin: {
			top: '5mm',
			right: '5mm',
			bottom: '10mm',
			left: '5mm',
        },
        format: 'A4',
        printBackground: true,
    });
 
}
 
// Generate OnePM FSR Child Report
const generateOnePMFSRChild = async (page, finalObject, pageNumber, totalPageNumber) => {
	await page.evaluate(({ finalObject, pageNumber, totalPageNumber }) => {
        function setData(el, data) {
            document.getElementById(el).textContent = data;
        }
 
        function setImage(el, data) {
            document.getElementById(el).src = data;
        }
 
        function setHTML(el, data) {
            document.getElementById(el).innerHTML = data;
        }
		setData('fsrNumber', finalObject?.fsr_number ? finalObject.fsr_number : finalObject.param.fsr_number || '');
		setData('fsrDateAndTime', finalObject.param.completion_date || '');
		setData('serviceType', finalObject.param.servicetype || '');
		setData('serviceProvider', finalObject.service_provider || '');
		// setData('serviceBillable', finalObject.param.fsr_number || '');
		setData('problemCode', finalObject.param.problem_code_description || '');
		setData('customerName', finalObject.param.customer_name || '');
		setData(
			'customerAddress',
			`${finalObject.param.customer_address1 ? finalObject.param.customer_address1 : ""}${finalObject.param.customer_address2 ? ', ' + finalObject.param.customer_address2 : ""}${finalObject.param.customer_address3 ? ', ' +  finalObject.param.customer_address3 : ""}`,
		);
		setData('contactPerson', finalObject.param.contact || '');
		setData('contactNumber', finalObject.param.contact_no || '');
		setData('branch', finalObject.param.servicebranch || '');
		setData('requestOwner', finalObject?.request_owner ? finalObject?.request_owner : finalObject.param?.request_owner || '');
		setData('requestDateAndTime', finalObject.call_log_date ? finalObject.call_log_date : finalObject.param.call_log_date || '');
		setData('assignedDateTime', finalObject.call_assigned_date ? finalObject.call_assigned_date : finalObject.param.call_assigned_date || '');
		setData('scheduledDateTime', finalObject.call_actual_schedule_date ? finalObject.call_actual_schedule_date :  finalObject.param.call_actual_schedule_date || '');
		setData('siteEngineer', finalObject?.engineername ? finalObject?.engineername : finalObject.param?.engineername || '');
		setData('engineerContact', finalObject.param.call_engineer_mobilenumber || '');
		setData('productModel', finalObject.param.product_model || '');
		setData('productRating', finalObject.param.product_rating || '');
		setData('serialNumber', finalObject.param.product_serialno || '');
		setData('contractNumber', finalObject.param.contract_no || '');
		setData('requestNumber', finalObject.param.call_no || '');
		setData('problemCode', finalObject.param.problem_code_description || '');
		setData('resolutionCode', finalObject.param.resolution_code_description || '');
		setData('problemStatement', finalObject.param.problemstatement || '');
		setData('resolutionSummary', finalObject?.resolution_summary ? finalObject.resolution_summary : finalObject.param?.resolution_summary || '');
		setData('travelStartDateAndTime', finalObject.param.travel_start_time || '');
		setData('reportingDateAndTime', finalObject.param.reporting_date || '');
		setData('completionDateAndTime', finalObject.param.completion_date || '');
		setData('onSiteTime', finalObject.param.on_site_time || '');
		setData('travelTime', finalObject.param.travel_time || '');
		setData('numberOfVisits', finalObject.param.visits || '');
		setData('equipmentFace', finalObject.param.equipment_facetime_info || '');
		setData('breakTime', finalObject.param.break_time || '');
		setData('totalTimeSpent', finalObject.param.total_time || '');
		setHTML('partConsumed', finalObject.issuedEls || '');
		setHTML('partReturned', finalObject.returnedEls || '');
		setHTML('pageNo', `<div><b>Page ${pageNumber} of</b></div> <div><b>${totalPageNumber}</b></div>`);
 
		let obContent = '';
		let wdContent = '';
		let reContent = '';
 
		for (let i = 0; i < finalObject.param.workbench.length; i++) {
			const workbenchObj = finalObject.param.workbench[i];
			if (workbenchObj.activity_type_value?.toLowerCase().indexOf('observation') !== -1) {
				if (workbenchObj.activity_notes?.length > 0) {
					obContent = obContent.length > 0 ? `${obContent}\n` : obContent;
					obContent = `${obContent}[${workbenchObj.activity_date}] &nbsp;${workbenchObj.activity_notes}`;
				}
			} else if (workbenchObj.activity_type_value?.toLowerCase().indexOf('recommendation') !== -1) {
				if (workbenchObj.activity_notes?.length > 0) {
					reContent = reContent.length > 0 ? `${reContent}\n` : reContent;
					reContent = `${reContent}[${workbenchObj.activity_date}] &nbsp;${workbenchObj.activity_notes}`;
				}
			} else if (workbenchObj.activity_type_value?.toLowerCase().indexOf('work done') !== -1) {
				if (workbenchObj.activity_notes?.length > 0) {
					wdContent = wdContent.length > 0 ? `${wdContent}\n` : wdContent;
					wdContent = `${wdContent}[${workbenchObj.activity_date}] &nbsp;${workbenchObj.activity_notes}`;
				}
			}
 
			setHTML('observation', obContent.replace(/\n/g, "<br><div style='height: 5px;'></div>"));
			setHTML('workDone', wdContent.replace(/\n/g, "<br><div style='height: 5px;'></div>"));
			setHTML('recommendation', reContent.replace(/\n/g, "<br><div style='height: 5px;'></div>"));
		}
 
		let signatureContent = `
			<div
			style="
				display: flex;
				flex-direction: row;
				text-align: center;
			"
			>
			<div style="width: 50%; flex-direction: column; height: inherit; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; text-align: center;">
				${finalObject?.customerSignature ? ` <img src="${finalObject.customerSignature}" id="customerSignatureAndSeal" class="full" /> ` : `<div class='full'></div>`}
				<div class="times-bold" style="border-top: 1px solid black;">Customer Signature</div>
			</div>
			<div style="width: 50%; flex-direction: column; height: inherit; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; text-align: center;  border-right: '0px' solid black; border-left: 1px solid black;">
				${finalObject?.engineerSignature ? `<img src="${finalObject?.engineerSignature}" id="engineerSignatureAndSeal" class="full" />` : `<div class='full'></div>`}
				<div class="times-bold" style="border-top: 1px solid black;">Engineer Signature</div>
			</div>
			</div>
		`
		setHTML('signatureContent', signatureContent);
	}, { finalObject, pageNumber, totalPageNumber });
 
	return await page.pdf({
        margin: {
            top: '14.5mm',
            right: '5mm',
            bottom: '10mm',
            left: '5mm',
        },
        format: 'A4',
        printBackground: true,
    });
 
}
